- **Active**: The originating process is still ongoing. The material is in the middle of its formation event. Molten lava, living wood, fresh blood, actively growing crystal. - **[Infused]**: Mundane action enhanced by magical energy (ÆP cost). [Source: AC Unified Architecture.md]
- **[Magical]**: Learned magical technique. Feeds Tₘ in ÆP pool formula. [Source: AC Unified Architecture.md]
- **[Mundane]**: No magical component. Physical/mental training only. [Source: AC Unified Architecture.md]
- **[Passive]**: Always on, no action required. [Source: AC Unified Architecture.md]
- **[Reactive]**: Uses reaction. [Source: AC Unified Architecture.md]
- **[Stance]**: Persistent state, one active at a time, switch as swift action. [Source: AC Unified Architecture.md]
- **[Supernatural]**: Innate magical ability tied to the body. Future mutation catalogue candidate. [Source: AC Unified Architecture.md]
- **[Swift]**: Uses swift/minor action. [Source: AC Unified Architecture.md]
